# LoadEhr parameter hierarchy (W10; regenerated W11)

Generated by `tools/param_audit.py map` from the static audit (`docs/audit/static.json`), the harness sweeps
(`docs/audit/sweep_<bundle>.json`) and the gates read from the code (`tools/param_audit_gates.json`). Regenerate after any
param change: `tools/param_audit.py all`.

**Visibility rule** (one rule for every param): >= 130 px (0.1 % of a 480x270 frame) change by > 8/255 in some channel vs the
param at its default, AND > 3x the default-vs-default noise of the same scene. Scenes: **S** skull, still; **M** S + Spin Y 30 +
Wobble Amount 1 + Pos X 0.4; **G** M + the param's gates opened. Numbers = the best test value per scene (changed px, noise px).

## Counts

| bundle | NO VISIBLE EFFECT | NON-VISUAL | VISIBLE ALWAYS | VISIBLE ONLY WHEN | VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LoadEhr | 1 | 12 | 111 | 323 | 40 |
| LoadEhrFX | 1 | 12 | 113 | 339 | 43 |
| LoadEhrWow | 2 | 12 | 86 | 367 | 30 |
| LoadEhrWowFX | 3 | 12 | 87 | 378 | 30 |

## LoadEhr


### Model

- **Model Source** — on its own. Slot A shows extruded text (or a logo) instead of the Model A file. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 28215 px (noise 0); M 1: 31204 px (noise 0); G 1: 31204 px (noise 0)`
- **Model A** — on its own. Which model file slot A loads and draws. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 26914 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 31787 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 31787 px (noise 0)`
- **Status A** — non-visual: read-only status line. None on screen: a text line telling what slot A holds. *NON-VISUAL* ``
- **Reload A** — non-visual: re-reads the same file: a load + swap of the same picture. Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 563 px (noise 0)`
- **Model B** — when the model moves. Which model file slot B loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

- **Reload B** — non-visual: re-reads the same file: a load + swap of the same picture. Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 563 px (noise 0)`
- **Model C** — when the model moves. Which model file slot C loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 1622 px (noise 0)`
- **Model D** — when the model moves. Which model file slot D loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 1622 px (noise 0)`
- **Auto Fit** — on its own. Off draws the model at its raw file size and position instead of centred and fitted to ~2 units. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 0: 6457 px (noise 0); M 0: 9928 px (noise 0); G 0: 9928 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fit To** — needs Model A = Flight Helmet, Part 7 Visible = 0, Part 8 Visible = 0. Visible Parts re-fits (centre / scale) to only the visible parts; All Parts keeps the whole-model fit. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 13465 px (noise 0)`
- **Normal Flip Y** — on its own. Inverts the normal map's green channel: surface bumps lit from the opposite vertical direction. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 3282 px (noise 0); M 1: 2978 px (noise 0); G 1: 2978 px (noise 0)`
- **Max Texture Size** — when the model moves. Downsamples big textures at load (less detail up close, less GPU memory). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 23817 px (noise 0); G 0: 77558 px (noise 0)`
- **Transition** — on its own. Morphs slot A into slot B (default Dissolve); 1 = the helmet. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 27737 px (noise 0); M 1: 37068 px (noise 0); G 1: 37068 px (noise 0)`
  - **Chain Loop** — needs Transition = 0.5. Adds a hop from the last model back to the first (the chain becomes a loop). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 26064 px (noise 0)`
  - **Transition Style** — needs Transition = 0.5. Picks how the outgoing model turns into the incoming one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 67623 px (noise 0)`
  - **Style Order** — needs Chain Loop = 1, Transition = 0.75. Chooses each hop's style: fixed, the next style each hop, or a hashed random one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 37319 px (noise 0)`
- **Transition Play** — on its own. The chain plays by itself over time (once / ping-pong / loop), ignoring the Transition slider. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 17267 px (noise 0); M 1: 26806 px (noise 0); G 1: 26806 px (noise 0)`
  - **Transition Time** — needs Transition Play = 3. Seconds per hop while the chain plays. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 34537 px (noise 0)`
  - **Hold Time** — needs Transition Play = 3, Transition Time = 1. Seconds the played chain rests on each model between hops. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 37068 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Ease** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.3. Shapes the hop's timing curve (0 = linear all-at-once flight, 1 = smoothstepped). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34960 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Stagger** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.3. Spreads the pieces' departures over the hop (rim first ... centre last). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 37684 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Spread** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.5. How far the shards fly (and the size / turns of the other styles). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 20380 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Sparkle** — needs Transition = 0.5. Twinkling glowing sparkles on both models around the swap point of the hop. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 22595 px (noise 0)`

- **Curve Detail** — when the model moves. Curve flattening tolerance of text / logo outlines (low = faceted curves). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 25798 px (noise 0); G 1: 58189 px (noise 0)`

### Letters

  - **Type On** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO". Letters type on left to right, rising into place (below 1 the later letters are hidden / lower). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 4496 px (noise 0)`
  - **Type Speed** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO". The text types itself on letter by letter, holds 2 s, repeats. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 1158 px (noise 0)`
  - **Letter Stagger** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO", Type On = 0.5. How much the letters' type-on (and hops) trail one another. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 2787 px (noise 0)`
  - **Letter Wave** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO". Letters bob up and down in a travelling wave. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 7610 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wave Speed** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO", Letter Wave = 1. Pace of the letter wave (cycles / s). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 7532 px (noise 0)`
  - **Letter Spin** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO". Each letter spins about its own vertical axis (deg / s). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G -30: 4691 px (noise 0)`
  - **Spin Mode** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO", Letter Spin = 30. Together = all letters spin alike; Separate = per-letter speed factors -2/-1/1/2. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 2390 px (noise 0)`
  - **Letter Hop** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO", a beat. Letters hop up one after another on every (1/2/4/8th) beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 1337 px (noise 0)`
  - **Hop Height** — needs Model Source = 1, Text = "HELLO", a beat, Letter Hop = 1. Height of the letter hops. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 3482 px (noise 0)`

### Shape

- **Shatter** — on its own. The model breaks into shards that fly out and fall. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S pulse: 35810 px (noise 0); M pulse: 11273 px (noise 0); G pulse: 11273 px (noise 0)`
- **Reform** — when the model moves. Shards fly back along their paths and the model reassembles over Reform Time. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 31003 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter On Beat** — on its own. Shatters on every Nth beat and reforms within the cycle. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 25468 px (noise 0); M 1: 32151 px (noise 0); G 1: 32151 px (noise 0)`
- **Shard Count** — when the model moves. More / fewer, smaller / bigger shards. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 8: 0 px (noise 0); M 8: 28575 px (noise 0); G 8: 1128 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter Mode** — when the model moves. Letters fly whole vs break into solid pieces. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 28575 px (noise 0); G 0: 0 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Force** — needs press Shatter. Shards fly out faster / farther. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 6085 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Gravity** — needs press Shatter. Shards fall faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34294 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shard Spin** — needs press Shatter. Shards tumble faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 20: 6901 px (noise 0)`
  - **Reform Time** — needs press Shatter, a moment (40 frames), press Reform. How long the shards take to fly home. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 60913 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Physics** — needs press Shatter. Shards bounce and slide on the floor instead of falling through. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 17465 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bounciness** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards rebound higher off the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32722 px (noise 0)`
  - **Floor Friction** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards slide / roll less on the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32833 px (noise 0)`
  - **Slice Count** — needs Slice Gap = 0.6. Number of slabs the model is cut into. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 2: 0 px (noise 0); M 2: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 34073 px (noise 0)`
- **Slice Gap** — on its own. Model splits into slabs with gaps between them. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 31730 px (noise 0); M 2: 40407 px (noise 0); G 2: 40407 px (noise 0)`
  - **Slice Axis** — needs Slice Gap = 0.6. Slabs are cut along X / Y / Z. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 52381 px (noise 0)`
- **Slice Wave** — on its own. Slabs slide back and forth in a wave. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 46041 px (noise 0); M 4: 30448 px (noise 0); G 4: 30448 px (noise 0)`
- **Voxel** — on its own. Vertices snap to a 3D grid: blocky model. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 22059 px (noise 0); M 1: 26544 px (noise 0); G 1: 26544 px (noise 0)`
  - **Voxel Size** — needs Voxel = 1. Bigger / smaller blocks. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.02: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.02: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.3: 27067 px (noise 0)`
- **Low Poly** — on its own. Vertices snap to a rotated grid: faceted model. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 24781 px (noise 0); M 1: 29413 px (noise 0); G 1: 29413 px (noise 0)`
  - **Low Poly Size** — needs Low Poly = 1. Bigger / smaller facets. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 30512 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Rot X** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid turns, changing the facet pattern. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 45: 0 px (noise 0); M 45: 0 px (noise 0); G 90: 31888 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Rot Y** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid turns, changing the facet pattern. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 45: 0 px (noise 0); M 45: 0 px (noise 0); G 90: 32860 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Rot Z** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid turns, changing the facet pattern. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 45: 0 px (noise 0); M 45: 0 px (noise 0); G 45: 34416 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Spin X** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid rotates continuously, facets crawl. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 31819 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Spin Y** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid rotates continuously, facets crawl. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 28995 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Spin Z** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid rotates continuously, facets crawl. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 34261 px (noise 0)`
  - **Facet Tumble** — needs Low Poly = 1, Tumble Y = 30. Facets rotate about their own centres. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 30130 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble X** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 1. Facets spin about that axis. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 33975 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble Y** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 1. Facets spin about that axis. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 28663 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble Z** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 1. Facets spin about that axis. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 34743 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble Random** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 2, Tumble Y = 30. Facets tumble on their own axes / speeds instead of together. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 33496 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble Sway** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 1, Tumble Y = 30. Facets rock back and forth instead of turning steadily. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 25023 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble On Beat** — needs Low Poly = 1. Facets flip 90/180 deg on the beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30158 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tumble Beat Flip** — needs Low Poly = 1, Tumble On Beat = 1. Each beat flip turns 180 instead of 90 deg. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 36316 px (noise 0)`
  - **Facet Gap** — needs Low Poly = 1. Facets shrink toward their centres, gaps between them. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 34555 px (noise 0)`
- **Tumble Reset** — when the model moves. Facets glide back to rest over 1 s. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 34632 px (noise 0)`
- **Blow To Dust** — on its own. The model blows away into a cloud of dust points over 2.5 s. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S pulse: 43674 px (noise 0); M pulse: mean shift 33.28/255 (noise 9.12; 37848 px vs 13445); G pulse: 38734 px (noise 15197)`
- **Dust Reform** — when the model moves. Dust flies back and the model reassembles. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 41500 px (noise 9384)`
- **Dust On Beat** — on its own. Blows to dust and reforms every Nth beat. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 542 px (noise 0); M 3: 7202 px (noise 0); G 3: 7202 px (noise 0)`
  - **Dust Hold** — needs press Blow To Dust. After the hold the dust reforms by itself (0 = stays out). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32465 px (noise 8184)`
- **Dust** — on its own. Scrubs the model into dust by hand. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 50000 px (noise 0); M 1: 53310 px (noise 7224); G 1: 53316 px (noise 7285)`
  - **Dust Count** — needs Dust = 0.6. More / fewer dust points. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 61714 px (noise 0)`
  - **Dust Wind** — needs Dust = 0.6. Dust drifts / swirls more. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34804 px (noise 0)`
  - **Dust Size** — needs Dust = 0.6. Bigger dust points. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.3: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.3: 0 px (noise 0); G 8: 63709 px (noise 0)`
- **Contours** — on its own. Glowing contour lines on the surface. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 10: 26967 px (noise 0); M 10: 31640 px (noise 0); G 10: 31640 px (noise 0)`
  - **Contour Mode** — needs Contours = 5. Lines along X / Z / height map / radial instead of Y. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 28039 px (noise 0)`
  - **Contour Spacing** — needs Contours = 5. Lines closer together / farther apart. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.02: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.02: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.3: 27734 px (noise 0)`
  - **Contour Width** — needs Contours = 5. Thicker / thinner lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.8: 30065 px (noise 0)`
  - **Contour Speed** — needs Contours = 5. Lines scroll along the model. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 29760 px (noise 0)`
  - **Contour Color** — needs Contours = 5. Line hue changes. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.33: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.33: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.66: 31630 px (noise 0)`

### Deform

  - **Deform Amount** — needs Twist = 2. Scales every deform. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30149 px (noise 0)`
  - **Deform Axis** — needs Twist = 2. Deforms run along X / Z instead of Y. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 36819 px (noise 0)`
- **Taper** — on its own. Model widens / narrows along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 32088 px (noise 0); M 2: 38343 px (noise 0); G 2: 38343 px (noise 0)`
- **Twist** — on its own. Model twists round the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S -8: 29931 px (noise 0); M 8: 31517 px (noise 0); G 8: 31517 px (noise 0)`
- **Bend** — on its own. Model bends. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 29205 px (noise 0); M -4: 36530 px (noise 0); G -4: 36530 px (noise 0)`
- **Squash** — on its own. Model squashed / stretched along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 32125 px (noise 0); M 1: 38508 px (noise 0); G 1: 38508 px (noise 0)`
- **Squash Beat** — on its own. Squash kick on each beat. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 29335 px (noise 0); M 2: 35390 px (noise 0); G 2: 35390 px (noise 0)`
- **Shear** — on its own. Model slides sideways along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 30735 px (noise 0); M 2: 40442 px (noise 0); G 2: 40442 px (noise 0)`
- **Bulge** — on its own. Middle bulges out / pinches in. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 55208 px (noise 0); M 2: 58822 px (noise 0); G 2: 58822 px (noise 0)`
- **Spherize** — on its own. Model morphs toward a sphere. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 25934 px (noise 0); M 1: 29702 px (noise 0); G 1: 29702 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shape Target** — needs Spherize = 1. Spherize morphs toward a cube instead of a sphere (W11: Torus / Crystal retired). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32593 px (noise 0)`
- **Noise Warp** — on its own. Smooth noise warps the surface. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 33401 px (noise 0); M 2: 40226 px (noise 0); G 2: 40226 px (noise 0)`
  - **Warp Scale** — needs Noise Warp = 1. Finer / coarser warp noise. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 8: 38200 px (noise 0)`
  - **Warp Speed** — needs Noise Warp = 1. Warp noise drifts faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 31559 px (noise 0)`
- **Ripple** — on its own. Ripples run over the surface. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 0.3: 36487 px (noise 0); M 0.3: 36410 px (noise 0); G 0.3: 36410 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Freq** — needs Ripple = 0.3. More / fewer ripple rings. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 3: 0 px (noise 0); M 3: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 51652 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Speed** — needs Ripple = 0.3. Ripples travel faster / reverse. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -20: 0 px (noise 0); M -20: 0 px (noise 0); G -20: 30746 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Beat** — needs Ripple = 0.3. Ripple amplitude kicks on the beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 4: 0 px (noise 0); M 4: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 126488 px (noise 0)`
- **Melt** — on its own. Model sags and melts down. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 33970 px (noise 0); M 1: 42356 px (noise 0); G 1: 42356 px (noise 0)`

### Wireframe

- **Wire Mode** — on its own. Wireframe over / instead of the surface, or points. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 27317 px (noise 0); M 4: 31982 px (noise 0); G 4: 31982 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wireframe Mix** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Line strength. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Thickness** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Thicker / thinner lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 22138 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Back** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Back-facing lines shown through (X-Ray). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 27796 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Edges** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Only crease / open edges drawn. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 29689 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line hue. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Sat** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour saturation. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bri** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour brightness. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.2: 30650 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color Mix** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines take the picked colour instead of the surface colour. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30665 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Glow** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines glow past white. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 30440 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Halo round the lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 38824 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom Size** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Bloom = 5. Wider / tighter halo. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 20792 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Fade** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines fade with distance from the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30097 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Source** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points at vertices / feature vertices / even surface density. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 28586 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Twinkle** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points flicker individually. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 22665 px (noise 0)`

- **Clone Layout** — on its own [clones]. Ring / Grid / Tunnel / Carousel copies of the mask appear. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 64027 px (noise 0); M 4: 79793 px (noise 0); G 1: 15847 px (noise 0)`

  - **Clone Spacing** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Distance between copies. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.2: 16034 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Spin Step** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Each copy turned a further step about its Y axis. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -180: 0 px (noise 0); M -180: 0 px (noise 0); G -180: 8592 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Hue Spread** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Copies' colours hue-shifted along the row. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 10947 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Orbit** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Ring / carousel turns, tunnel rings fly toward the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -30: 0 px (noise 0); M -30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 28778 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Scale** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Size of each copy. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 118679 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tunnel Fade** — needs Clone Layout = 3, Distance = 9 [clones]. Tunnel rings dissolve in at the back and out at the front. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 3665 px (noise 0)`
  - **Keep Original** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Shows / hides the original mask among the copies. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 5993 px (noise 0)`
  - **LOD Bias** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Swaps copies to finer / coarser simplified meshes (subtle). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 10900 px (noise 0)`
- **Trail Echoes** — on its own [echoes]. Feedback trail of past frames behind the live mask. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 7: 13305 px (noise 0); M 7: 10993 px (noise 0); G 7: 10793 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trail Delay** — needs Trail Echoes = 7, Trail Offset X = 0.05 [echoes]. Frames between stamped copies (spacing and trail length). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 21011 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trail Decay** — needs Trail Echoes = 7, Trail Offset X = 0.05 [echoes]. How fast older echoes fade. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 22288 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trail Offset X** — needs Trail Echoes = 7 [echoes]. Each echo slides sideways (screen width per Delay). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M -0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G -0.1: 54307 px (noise 695)`
  - **Trail Offset Y** — needs Trail Echoes = 7 [echoes]. Each echo slides up/down. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M -0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G -0.1: 20786 px (noise 695)`
- **Ghosts** — on its own [ghosts]. Translucent copies of past poses behind the mask. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 8: 1162 px (noise 0); M 8: 8631 px (noise 0); G 8: 8631 px (noise 0)`

## LoadEhrFX


### Model

- **Model Source** — on its own. Slot A shows extruded text (or a logo) instead of the Model A file. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 28215 px (noise 0); M 1: 31204 px (noise 0); G 1: 31204 px (noise 0)`
- **Model A** — on its own. Which model file slot A loads and draws. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 26914 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 31787 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 31787 px (noise 0)`
- **Status A** — non-visual: read-only status line. None on screen: a text line telling what slot A holds. *NON-VISUAL* ``
- **Reload A** — non-visual: re-reads the same file: a load + swap of the same picture. Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 563 px (noise 0)`
- **Model B** — when the model moves. Which model file slot B loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

- **Model C** — when the model moves. Which model file slot C loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 1622 px (noise 0)`
- **Model D** — when the model moves. Which model file slot D loads; it joins the morph chain. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S Skull/scene.gltf: 0 px (noise 0); M Skull/scene.gltf: 18161 px (noise 0); G Skull/scene.gltf: 33138 px (noise 0)`

Re-reads the slot's file from disk; the old model keeps drawing until the reload swaps in (no visible change for an unchanged file). *NON-VISUAL* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 563 px (noise 0); G pulse: 1622 px (noise 0)`
- **Auto Fit** — on its own. Off draws the model at its raw file size and position instead of centred and fitted to ~2 units. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 0: 6457 px (noise 0); M 0: 9928 px (noise 0); G 0: 9928 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fit To** — needs Model A = Flight Helmet, Part 7 Visible = 0, Part 8 Visible = 0. Visible Parts re-fits (centre / scale) to only the visible parts; All Parts keeps the whole-model fit. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 13465 px (noise 0)`
- **Normal Flip Y** — on its own. Inverts the normal map's green channel: surface bumps lit from the opposite vertical direction. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 3282 px (noise 0); M 1: 2978 px (noise 0); G 1: 2978 px (noise 0)`
- **Max Texture Size** — when the model moves. Downsamples big textures at load (less detail up close, less GPU memory). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 23817 px (noise 0); G 0: 77558 px (noise 0)`
- **Transition** — on its own. Morphs slot A into slot B (default Dissolve); 1 = the helmet. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 27737 px (noise 0); M 1: 37068 px (noise 0); G 1: 37068 px (noise 0)`
  - **Chain Loop** — needs Transition = 0.5. Adds a hop from the last model back to the first (the chain becomes a loop). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 26064 px (noise 0)`
  - **Transition Style** — needs Transition = 0.5. Picks how the outgoing model turns into the incoming one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 67623 px (noise 0)`
  - **Style Order** — needs Chain Loop = 1, Transition = 0.75. Chooses each hop's style: fixed, the next style each hop, or a hashed random one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 37319 px (noise 0)`
- **Transition Play** — on its own. The chain plays by itself over time (once / ping-pong / loop), ignoring the Transition slider. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 17267 px (noise 0); M 1: 26806 px (noise 0); G 1: 26806 px (noise 0)`
  - **Transition Time** — needs Transition Play = 3. Seconds per hop while the chain plays. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 34537 px (noise 0)`
  - **Hold Time** — needs Transition Play = 3, Transition Time = 1. Seconds the played chain rests on each model between hops. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 37068 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Ease** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.3. Shapes the hop's timing curve (0 = linear all-at-once flight, 1 = smoothstepped). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34960 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Stagger** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.3. Spreads the pieces' departures over the hop (rim first ... centre last). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 37684 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Spread** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.5. How far the shards fly (and the size / turns of the other styles). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 20380 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Sparkle** — needs Transition = 0.5. Twinkling glowing sparkles on both models around the swap point of the hop. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 22595 px (noise 0)`

### Shape

- **Shatter** — on its own. The model breaks into shards that fly out and fall. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S pulse: 35810 px (noise 0); M pulse: 11273 px (noise 0); G pulse: 11273 px (noise 0)`
- **Reform** — when the model moves. Shards fly back along their paths and the model reassembles over Reform Time. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 31003 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter On Beat** — on its own. Shatters on every Nth beat and reforms within the cycle. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 25468 px (noise 0); M 1: 32151 px (noise 0); G 1: 32151 px (noise 0)`
- **Shard Count** — when the model moves. More / fewer, smaller / bigger shards. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 8: 0 px (noise 0); M 8: 28575 px (noise 0); G 8: 1128 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter Mode** — when the model moves. Letters fly whole vs break into solid pieces. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 28575 px (noise 0); G 0: 0 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Force** — needs press Shatter. Shards fly out faster / farther. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 6085 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Gravity** — needs press Shatter. Shards fall faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34294 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shard Spin** — needs press Shatter. Shards tumble faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 20: 6901 px (noise 0)`
  - **Reform Time** — needs press Shatter, a moment (40 frames), press Reform. How long the shards take to fly home. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 60913 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Physics** — needs press Shatter. Shards bounce and slide on the floor instead of falling through. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 17465 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bounciness** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards rebound higher off the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32722 px (noise 0)`
  - **Floor Friction** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards slide / roll less on the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32833 px (noise 0)`
  - **Slice Count** — needs Slice Gap = 0.6. Number of slabs the model is cut into. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 2: 0 px (noise 0); M 2: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 34073 px (noise 0)`
- **Slice Gap** — on its own. Model splits into slabs with gaps between them. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 31730 px (noise 0); M 2: 40407 px (noise 0); G 2: 40407 px (noise 0)`
  - **Slice Axis** — needs Slice Gap = 0.6. Slabs are cut along X / Y / Z. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 52381 px (noise 0)`
- **Slice Wave** — on its own. Slabs slide back and forth in a wave. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 46041 px (noise 0); M 4: 30448 px (noise 0); G 4: 30448 px (noise 0)`
- **Voxel** — on its own. Vertices snap to a 3D grid: blocky model. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 22059 px (noise 0); M 1: 26544 px (noise 0); G 1: 26544 px (noise 0)`

  - **LowPoly Rot Y** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid turns, changing the facet pattern. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 45: 0 px (noise 0); M 45: 0 px (noise 0); G 90: 32860 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Rot Z** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid turns, changing the facet pattern. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 45: 0 px (noise 0); M 45: 0 px (noise 0); G 45: 34416 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Spin X** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid rotates continuously, facets crawl. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 31819 px (noise 0)`
  - **LowPoly Spin Y** — needs Low Poly = 1. The facet grid rotates continuously, facets crawl. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 30: 0 px (noise 0); M 30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 28995 px (noise 0)`

  - **Tumble Random** — needs Low Poly = 1, Facet Tumble = 2, Tumble Y = 30. Facets tumble on their own axes / speeds instead of together. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 33496 px (noise 0)`

### Deform

  - **Deform Amount** — needs Twist = 2. Scales every deform. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30149 px (noise 0)`
  - **Deform Axis** — needs Twist = 2. Deforms run along X / Z instead of Y. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 36819 px (noise 0)`
- **Taper** — on its own. Model widens / narrows along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 32088 px (noise 0); M 2: 38343 px (noise 0); G 2: 38343 px (noise 0)`
- **Twist** — on its own. Model twists round the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S -8: 29931 px (noise 0); M 8: 31517 px (noise 0); G 8: 31517 px (noise 0)`
- **Bend** — on its own. Model bends. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 29205 px (noise 0); M -4: 36530 px (noise 0); G -4: 36530 px (noise 0)`
- **Squash** — on its own. Model squashed / stretched along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 32125 px (noise 0); M 1: 38508 px (noise 0); G 1: 38508 px (noise 0)`
- **Squash Beat** — on its own. Squash kick on each beat. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 29335 px (noise 0); M 2: 35390 px (noise 0); G 2: 35390 px (noise 0)`
- **Shear** — on its own. Model slides sideways along the axis. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 30735 px (noise 0); M 2: 40442 px (noise 0); G 2: 40442 px (noise 0)`
- **Bulge** — on its own. Middle bulges out / pinches in. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 55208 px (noise 0); M 2: 58822 px (noise 0); G 2: 58822 px (noise 0)`
- **Spherize** — on its own. Model morphs toward a sphere. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 25934 px (noise 0); M 1: 29702 px (noise 0); G 1: 29702 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shape Target** — needs Spherize = 1. Spherize morphs toward a cube instead of a sphere (W11: Torus / Crystal retired). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 32593 px (noise 0)`
- **Noise Warp** — on its own. Smooth noise warps the surface. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 2: 33401 px (noise 0); M 2: 40226 px (noise 0); G 2: 40226 px (noise 0)`
  - **Warp Scale** — needs Noise Warp = 1. Finer / coarser warp noise. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 8: 38200 px (noise 0)`
  - **Warp Speed** — needs Noise Warp = 1. Warp noise drifts faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 31559 px (noise 0)`
- **Ripple** — on its own. Ripples run over the surface. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 0.3: 36487 px (noise 0); M 0.3: 36410 px (noise 0); G 0.3: 36410 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Freq** — needs Ripple = 0.3. More / fewer ripple rings. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 3: 0 px (noise 0); M 3: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 51652 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Speed** — needs Ripple = 0.3. Ripples travel faster / reverse. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -20: 0 px (noise 0); M -20: 0 px (noise 0); G -20: 30746 px (noise 0)`
  - **Ripple Beat** — needs Ripple = 0.3. Ripple amplitude kicks on the beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 4: 0 px (noise 0); M 4: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 126488 px (noise 0)`
- **Melt** — on its own. Model sags and melts down. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 33970 px (noise 0); M 1: 42356 px (noise 0); G 1: 42356 px (noise 0)`
  - **Drip** — needs Melt = 0.8. Drip columns run further ahead. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 23968 px (noise 0)`
- **Glitch Shift** — on its own. Bands jump sideways. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 5429 px (noise 0); M 1: 12690 px (noise 0); G 1: 12690 px (noise 0)`

### Wireframe

- **Wire Mode** — on its own. Wireframe over / instead of the surface, or points. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 27317 px (noise 0); M 4: 31982 px (noise 0); G 4: 31982 px (noise 0)`

  - **Wire Thickness** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Thicker / thinner lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 22138 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Back** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Back-facing lines shown through (X-Ray). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 27796 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Edges** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Only crease / open edges drawn. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 29689 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line hue. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Sat** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour saturation. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bri** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour brightness. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.2: 30650 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color Mix** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines take the picked colour instead of the surface colour. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30665 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Glow** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines glow past white. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 30440 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Halo round the lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 38824 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom Size** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Bloom = 5. Wider / tighter halo. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 20792 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Fade** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines fade with distance from the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30097 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Source** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points at vertices / feature vertices / even surface density. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 28586 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Twinkle** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points flicker individually. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 22665 px (noise 0)`

  - **Line Reach** — needs Line Look = 1. Longer plexus links / longer ribbons / wider neon light. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.05: 16278 px (noise 0)`
  - **Line Speed** — needs Line Look = 1. Pulses along plexus lines (light paint: swirl; neon: flow / flicker). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 8020 px (noise 0)`
  - **Line Audio** — needs Line Look = 1, audio (FFT). Lines pulse in reach / brightness with the audio bands. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 4: 0 px (noise 0); M 4: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 16094 px (noise 0)`

  - **Clone Spin Step** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Each copy turned a further step about its Y axis. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -180: 0 px (noise 0); M -180: 0 px (noise 0); G -180: 8592 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Hue Spread** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Copies' colours hue-shifted along the row. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 10947 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Orbit** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Ring / carousel turns, tunnel rings fly toward the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -30: 0 px (noise 0); M -30: 0 px (noise 0); G 30: 28778 px (noise 0)`
  - **Clone Scale** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Size of each copy. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 118679 px (noise 0)`
  - **Tunnel Fade** — needs Clone Layout = 3, Distance = 9 [clones]. Tunnel rings dissolve in at the back and out at the front. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 3665 px (noise 0)`
  - **Keep Original** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Shows / hides the original mask among the copies. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 5993 px (noise 0)`
  - **LOD Bias** — needs Clone Layout = 1, Distance = 9 [clones]. Swaps copies to finer / coarser simplified meshes (subtle). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 10900 px (noise 0)`

  - **Trail Decay** — needs Trail Echoes = 7, Trail Offset X = 0.05 [echoes]. How fast older echoes fade. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 22288 px (noise 0)`

  - **Transition Style** — needs Transition = 0.5. Picks how the outgoing model turns into the incoming one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 67623 px (noise 0)`
  - **Style Order** — needs Chain Loop = 1, Transition = 0.75. Chooses each hop's style: fixed, the next style each hop, or a hashed random one. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 37319 px (noise 0)`
- **Transition Play** — on its own. The chain plays by itself over time (once / ping-pong / loop), ignoring the Transition slider. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 17267 px (noise 0); M 1: 26806 px (noise 0); G 1: 26806 px (noise 0)`

  - **Hold Time** — needs Transition Play = 3, Transition Time = 1. Seconds the played chain rests on each model between hops. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 37068 px (noise 0)`
  - **Trans Ease** — needs Transition Style = 1, Transition = 0.3. Shapes the hop's timing curve (0 = linear all-at-once flight, 1 = smoothstepped). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 34960 px (noise 0)`

### Shape

- **Shatter** — on its own. The model breaks into shards that fly out and fall. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S pulse: 56338 px (noise 0); M pulse: 50936 px (noise 25394); G pulse: 50936 px (noise 25394)`
- **Reform** — when the model moves. Shards fly back along their paths and the model reassembles over Reform Time. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 65026 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter On Beat** — on its own. Shatters on every Nth beat and reforms within the cycle. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 15277 px (noise 0); M 1: 18399 px (noise 0); G 1: 18399 px (noise 0)`
- **Shard Count** — when the model moves. More / fewer, smaller / bigger shards. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 8: 0 px (noise 0); M 8: 28575 px (noise 0); G 512: 53842 px (noise 0)`
- **Shatter Mode** — when the model moves. Letters fly whole vs break into solid pieces. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 28575 px (noise 0); G 2: 11079 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Force** — needs press Shatter. Shards fly out faster / farther. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 58885 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Gravity** — needs press Shatter. Shards fall faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 62156 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shard Spin** — needs press Shatter. Shards tumble faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 50025 px (noise 0)`
  - **Reform Time** — needs press Shatter, a moment (40 frames), press Reform. How long the shards take to fly home. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 52813 px (noise 0)`
  - **Shatter Physics** — needs press Shatter. Shards bounce and slide on the floor instead of falling through. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 53392 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bounciness** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards rebound higher off the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 52261 px (noise 0)`
  - **Floor Friction** — needs Shatter Physics = 1, press Shatter. Shards slide / roll less on the floor. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 51719 px (noise 0)`
  - **Physics Speed** — needs press Shatter. Shard flight / reform runs slower or faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 65225 px (noise 0)`

- **Melt** — on its own. Model sags and melts down. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 33970 px (noise 0); M 1: 42356 px (noise 0); G 1: 42356 px (noise 0)`
  - **Drip** — needs Melt = 0.8. Drip columns run further ahead. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: 23968 px (noise 0)`
- **Glitch Shift** — on its own. Bands jump sideways. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 5429 px (noise 0); M 1: 12690 px (noise 0); G 1: 12690 px (noise 0)`
  - **Glitch Bands** — needs Glitch Shift = 1. More / fewer jumping bands. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 2: 0 px (noise 0); M 2: 0 px (noise 0); G 64: 25854 px (noise 0)`
  - **Glitch Rate** — needs Glitch Shift = 1. Bands jump more often. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 22252 px (noise 0)`
- **Mirror Fold** — on its own. Model folded into a kaleidoscope across centre planes. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 3: 27590 px (noise 0); M 3: 29604 px (noise 0); G 3: 29604 px (noise 0)`
- **Jelly** — when the model moves. Model lags and wobbles like jelly when it moves. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S 2: 1 px (noise 0); M 2: 1037 px (noise 0); G 2: 1037 px (noise 0)`
  - **Audio > Deform** — needs audio (FFT), Twist = 2. Audio level adds to Deform Amount. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 4: 0 px (noise 0); M 4: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 32000 px (noise 0)`

### Wireframe

  - **Wire Back** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Back-facing lines shown through (X-Ray). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 27796 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Edges** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Only crease / open edges drawn. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 29689 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line hue. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Sat** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour saturation. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 30716 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bri** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Color Mix = 1. Line colour brightness. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.2: 30650 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Color Mix** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines take the picked colour instead of the surface colour. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30665 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Glow** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines glow past white. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 30440 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Halo round the lines. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 38824 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Bloom Size** — needs Wire Mode = 2, Wire Bloom = 5. Wider / tighter halo. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 20792 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wire Fade** — needs Wire Mode = 2. Lines fade with distance from the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 30097 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Source** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points at vertices / feature vertices / even surface density. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 28586 px (noise 0)`
  - **Point Twinkle** — needs Wire Mode = 4. Points flicker individually. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 22665 px (noise 0)`

### Volume

  - **Vol Method** — needs B Enable = 1. Raymarched medium vs screen-space light rays. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 45794 px (noise 13)`
  - **Haze** — needs B Enable = 1. Uniform haze density in the light beams. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 34825 px (noise 13)`
  - **Smoke Amount** — needs Smoke Ambient = 1. Noise-smoke density. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 129600 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Scale** — needs Smoke Ambient = 1. Size of smoke features. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.1: 112303 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Speed** — needs Smoke Ambient = 1. Horizontal drift speed of the smoke. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 64404 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Turbulence** — needs Smoke Ambient = 1. Domain warp of the smoke. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 66942 px (noise 1)`
  - **Smoke Rise** — needs Smoke Ambient = 1. Vertical drift of the smoke. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -2: 0 px (noise 0); M -2: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 55115 px (noise 2)`
  - **Glow Cloud** — needs B Enable = 1. Glowing cloud around each volume light. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 21431 px (noise 21)`
- **Smoke Ambient** — on its own. Smoke lit everywhere in the key's hue. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 129600 px (noise 0); M 4: 129600 px (noise 0); G 4: 129600 px (noise 0)`
- **Smoke Key Light** — on its own. Smoke lit by the key light with the mask's shadow. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 4: 129600 px (noise 0); M 4: 129600 px (noise 0); G 4: 129600 px (noise 0)`
  - **Volume Size** — needs B Enable = 1. Radius of the medium sphere round the mask. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 47306 px (noise 8)`
  - **Scatter G** — needs B Enable = 1. Forward / back scattering of the beams. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -0.9: 0 px (noise 0); M -0.9: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.99: 40977 px (noise 9)`
  - **Shaft Length** — needs B Enable = 1. Distance range of the lights (surface and beams). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.2: 48318 px (noise 23)`
  - **Motes** — needs B Enable = 1. Dust motes sparkling in the beams. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 3635 px (noise 24)`
  - **Vol Quality** — needs B Enable = 1. March steps (16..128): smoother beams. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 412 px (noise 22)`
  - **Vol Res** — needs B Enable = 1. Fog buffer resolution. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 392 px (noise 19)`
  - **Fog Blend** — needs B Enable = 1. Add vs Over compositing of the lit medium. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 35013 px (noise 12)`
  - **Drift Return** — needs A Enable = 1, A Hue Drift = 0.3, A Sat = 1, Haze = 1. How long a drifted light hue takes to glide home. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 129600 px (noise 0)`

### Smoke Sim

  - **Smoke Engine** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Smoke Ambient = 1. Noise smoke vs a simulated plume rising off the mask; Sim + Detail adds fine noise detail. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 129600 px (noise 0)`
  - **Sim Res** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Grid resolution of the plume (coarse blobs at 48 vs fine wisps at 128); a change restarts the grid. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: mean shift 16.78/255 (noise 1.30; 98474 px vs 43091)`
  - **Sim Box** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Size of the sim grid round the mask; small box clips the plume. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 101582 px (noise 0)`
  - **Sim Iterations** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Pressure solve iterations: subtle change in the plume's swirl structure. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 10: 0 px (noise 0); M 10: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 8004 px (noise 0)`
  - **Buoyancy** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. How fast the hot smoke rises. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: mean shift 10.25/255 (noise 1.30; 65860 px vs 43091)`
  - **Heat Decay** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. How fast heat cools: high = smoke stops rising and pools. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 50189 px (noise 0)`
  - **Vorticity** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Curl confinement: more small swirls in the plume. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: mean shift 8.52/255 (noise 1.30; 90213 px vs 43091)`
  - **Smoke Fade** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. How fast the smoke density fades: high = short plume. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 108388 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wind X** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Pushes the plume along world X. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -3: 0 px (noise 0); M -3: 0 px (noise 0); G -3: 106214 px (noise 0)`
  - **Wind Y** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Pushes the plume along world Y. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -3: 0 px (noise 0); M -3: 0 px (noise 0); G 3: mean shift 11.62/255 (noise 1.30; 72130 px vs 43091)`
  - **Wind Z** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Pushes the plume along world Z. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S -3: 0 px (noise 0); M -3: 0 px (noise 0); G -3: mean shift 14.93/255 (noise 1.30; 76856 px vs 43091)`
  - **Sim Turbulence** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Noise forces breaking the plume up. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: mean shift 16.21/255 (noise 0.64; 91924 px vs 43781)`
  - **Emit Eyes** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1. Smoke jets out of the eye openings toward the camera. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 38135 px (noise 0)`
  - **Eyes Heat** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Eyes = 5. Heat of the eye jets: hotter = they rise. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 14638 px (noise 0)`
  - **Emit Behind** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1. Smoke from a blob behind the mask, rising round it. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 44594 px (noise 0)`
  - **Behind Heat** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Behind = 5. Heat of the behind source: hotter rises faster. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 15984 px (noise 0)`
  - **Emit Rim** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1. Smoke from the mask's silhouette band. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 110122 px (noise 0)`
  - **Rim Heat** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 5. Heat of the rim smoke. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 42540 px (noise 0)`
  - **Emit Inside** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1. Smoke from inside the model's cavity, leaking out of its openings (may be weak on a model without a closed cavity). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 365 px (noise 0)`
- **Inside Heat** — no visible effect (accepted: inside smoke stays in the closed cavity (skull: 1112 cavity voxels), so its heat only stirs it in there: from outside 0 px; with the mask hidden 15.6k px vs 15.0k px run-to-run sim noise. may show on a model with an open cavity). Heat of the inside smoke. *NO VISIBLE EFFECT (accepted: inside smoke stays in the closed cavity (skull: 1112 cavity voxels), so its heat only stirs it in there: from outside 0 px; with the mask hidden 15.6k px vs 15.0k px run-to-run sim noise. May show on a model with an open cavity)* `S 5: 0 px (noise 0); M 5: 0 px (noise 0); G 5: 0 px (noise 0)`
  - **Emit Offset** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 5. Rim / eye smoke starts that far out of the surface. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 67381 px (noise 0)`
  - **Beat Puff** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Bursts of extra smoke on the beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: mean shift 12.70/255 (noise 1.30; 61904 px vs 43091)`
  - **Puff Amount** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Beat Puff = 1. Size of the beat bursts. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: mean shift 14.56/255 (noise 0.11; 77021 px vs 54342)`
  - **Audio > Emit** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, audio (FFT). More smoke with louder bass. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 74006 px (noise 0)`
- **Sim Reset** — when the model moves. Clears the grid: the plume vanishes and regrows (prewarm 8 substeps / frame, so look within 1-2 frames). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: mean shift 6.07/255 (noise 0.05; 58252 px vs 45615)`
  - **Smoke Color** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Smoke Sat = 1. Hue of the light the sim smoke scatters. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 109073 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Sat** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Saturation of the smoke tint (hue 0 = red). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 111040 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Bri** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Brightness of the smoke tint (0 = black smoke). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: mean shift 36.42/255 (noise 1.94; 115873 px vs 41204)`
  - **Smoke Alpha** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. Density of the sim smoke (0 = none). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 117618 px (noise 0)`
  - **Smoke Confine** — needs Smoke Engine = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1, Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. A soft wall ~1.3 mask radii out keeps the smoke near the mask. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 82017 px (noise 0)`

### Fire

  - **Fire** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3. The emitters give burning fuel: blackbody flames rise off the mask. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 45697 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fire Speed** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Pace of the flames (soft vs frantic). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.1: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: mean shift 20.04/255 (noise 1.34; 64228 px vs 29373)`
  - **Burn Rate** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. How fast hot fuel burns: low = fuel smoke, high = short hot flames. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 39027 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Temp** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Flame colour red -> yellow -> white. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1000: 0 px (noise 0); M 1000: 0 px (noise 0); G 1000: 52902 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Height** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Taller flames (slower cooling, more lift). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 35785 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Glow** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Flame brightness. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 42630 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fire Smoke** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1, Smoke Ambient = 1. Soot smoke above the flames. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 42550 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Tint Color** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1, Flame Tint Mix = 1. Hue the flames are pulled toward. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 27775 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Tint Sat** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1, Flame Tint Mix = 1. Saturation of the flame tint. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 27770 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Tint Bri** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1, Flame Tint Mix = 1. Brightness of the flame tint. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 33330 px (noise 0)`
  - **Flame Tint Mix** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Blackbody colour vs the tint colour. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 33336 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fire Burst** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Fuel surges on the beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 34340 px (noise 0)`
  - **Burst Amount** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1, Fire Burst = 1. Size of the beat surges. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 56262 px (noise 0)`
  - **Fire Light** — needs Emit Rim = 3, Emit Behind = 3, Fire = 1. Flickering firelight on the mask surface. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 30696 px (noise 0)`

### Lightning

- **Lightning Strike** — when the model moves. One strike of bolts crawling over the mask, flickering out over Bolt Life. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 6582 px (noise 0)`
- **Strike On Beat** — on its own. A strike on every Nth beat. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 129600 px (noise 0); M 1: 129600 px (noise 0); G 1: 129600 px (noise 0)`
- **Strike Rate** — on its own. Strikes per second by themselves (crackle). *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 10: 129600 px (noise 0); M 10: 129600 px (noise 0); G 10: 129600 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Path** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolts crawl on the surface / arc eye to eye / drop to the floor (W11: To Clones retired, Wow has no clones). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 18641 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Count** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolts per strike. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 1: 0 px (noise 0); M 1: 0 px (noise 0); G 8: 14834 px (noise 0)`
  - **Branching** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Side branches off each bolt. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 12059 px (noise 0)`
  - **Jaggedness** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Zig-zag amplitude of the bolts. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 10138 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Life** — needs Strike Rate = 2. How long each strike flickers (more bolts on screen when long). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.05: 0 px (noise 0); G 2: 129571 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Width** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Core width of the bolts. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 6: 33691 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Glow** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Glow halo round the bolts. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 11077 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Color** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolt colour color. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 20558 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Sat** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolt colour sat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 20161 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Bri** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolt colour bri. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 128158 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Alpha** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Bolt brightness; 0 = no bolts at all. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 129600 px (noise 0)`
  - **Bolt Flash** — needs Strike Rate = 4, Bolt Life = 1. Each strike flashes light on the mask and in the haze/smoke. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 129057 px (noise 0)`

### Swarm

- **Swarm Count** — on its own. Glowing fireflies form the mask's shape. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 3: 4181 px (noise 0); M 3: 3068 px (noise 0); G 3: 3068 px (noise 0)`
- **Swarm Release** — when the model moves. The swarm bursts outward, then re-forms. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 6582 px (noise 0); G pulse: 15068 px (noise 3719)`
  - **Release On Beat** — needs Swarm Count = 3. The swarm bursts on every Nth beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 7230 px (noise 0)`
  - **Release Time** — needs Swarm Count = 3, press Swarm Release. How long the swarm stays scattered before re-forming. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 12401 px (noise 0)`
  - **Attraction** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Pull to the mask surface: 0 = they drift away. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 9487 px (noise 2564)`
  - **Flock** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Boids flocking strength (alignment / spacing). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 5066 px (noise 0)`
  - **Swarm Speed** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Curl-noise wander of the fireflies. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 4: 6322 px (noise 0)`
  - **Swarm Offset** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Shell distance of the swarm out from the surface. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 10069 px (noise 2564)`
  - **Firefly Size** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly dot size. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.5: 0 px (noise 0); G 8: 15671 px (noise 2564)`
  - **Firefly Glow** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly brightness. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 3084 px (noise 0)`
  - **Twinkle** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Fireflies blink. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 1126 px (noise 0)`
  - **Firefly Color** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly colour color. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0.5: 1928 px (noise 0)`
  - **Firefly Sat** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly colour sat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: mean shift 0.78/255 (noise 0.01; 1803 px vs 2570)`
  - **Firefly Bri** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly colour bri. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 3084 px (noise 0)`
  - **Firefly Alpha** — needs Swarm Count = 3. Firefly brightness; 0 = no swarm. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 3084 px (noise 0)`
- **Mask Visible** — on its own. Off hides the model (every part) so only the swarm / other effects show. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 0: 26969 px (noise 0); M 0: 31643 px (noise 0); G 0: 31643 px (noise 0)`

### Lava

- **Lava Cracks** — on its own. Glowing lava cracks spread over the mask. *VISIBLE ALWAYS* `S 1: 9076 px (noise 0); M 1: 11788 px (noise 0); G 1: 11798 px (noise 0)`
  - **Crack Spread** — needs Lava Cracks = 1. How far the cracks have spread (by hand). *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 0: 9829 px (noise 0)`
- **Crack Start** — when the model moves. Cracks run over the mask from a new seed in Crack Time. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 1992 px (noise 0); G pulse: 4447 px (noise 0)`
- **Crack Reverse** — when the model moves. The cracks heal back. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN MOVING* `S pulse: 0 px (noise 0); M pulse: 1992 px (noise 0); G pulse: 13226 px (noise 0)`
  - **Crack On Beat** — needs Lava Cracks = 1. Cracks open on the beat, then flare on each beat. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0: 0 px (noise 0); M 0: 0 px (noise 0); G 1: 5303 px (noise 0)`
  - **Crack Time** — needs Lava Cracks = 1, press Crack Start. How long the cracks take to spread. *VISIBLE ONLY WHEN* `S 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); M 0.2: 0 px (noise 0); G 10: 2580 px (noise 0)`
